It runs on Higgsfield models through MCP: pick a preset on the Katana page or in a Claude chat, add your references, and the skill generates the edit. Presets: a Higgsfield library in three categories, available on the site and in Claude. References: up to 5 images of characters, products, or clothing per edit. To adjust the result, switch on Prompt and describe the change. Community presets: share your own from the Katana page with a 3 to 30 second video for the motion, up to 40 images, and a prompt that mentions them with @. Shared presets go live right away and can be taken down after reports. To get started: open the Katana page → pick a preset → upload your images → Recreate in Claude. Or ask Claude directly: “make me an edit of this product”. Release MCP & Integrations Oct 8, 2026 Film Festival Audience Choice voting is open Vote for shortlisted films until October 15, 2026, 11:59 PM UTC. The film with the most votes wins the $100,000 prize. Shortlists: Create in Public, the top projects by community reactions (up to 50), and Higgsfield Picks, selected by the team. Likes from Create in Public don’t carry over; voting starts from zero. Votes: up to 5 per user, one per film, after watching it all the way through. A vote can be moved to another film anytime until voting closes. Prizes: Audience Choice is separate from the jury prizes for 1st to 3rd place and honorable mentions. One film can win only one prize. To get started: open the festival page → watch a shortlisted film → Vote. Availability: accounts with a paid plan. You can’t vote for your own film. Announcement Platform Oct 7, 2026 Higgsfield Discord 200K Contest A community contest to celebrate reaching 200,000 members on our Discord server. Take any viral video trend and make your own version of it on Higgsfield. The prize pool is 200,000 credits, and the community picks the winners by voting. Full rules and FAQ are in Discord. Feature Supercomputer Sep 22, 2026 Seedance 2.5 Draft Mode A two-step mode on Higgsfield: a 480p draft first, then a 1080p render of the one you choose. A draft costs fewer credits than a full-quality video, so testing several versions of one idea is cheaper. The final render keeps the original settings: prompt, references, seed, aspect ratio, and duration. Iterations: generate as many previews as you need, changing the text or images between them. First and last frame: upload a start image and an end image, and the model fills in the motion between them. Sound: voices, effects, and background noise are generated with the video, so you can check them before upgrading. Length: up to 30 seconds. To get started: Video → Seedance 2.5 Draft Mode → generate → select a preview → generate in 1080p. Availability: all regions except the US, where the provider doesn't offer the model. Design-grade images, vectors included Recraft v4.1 is built for professional design work, with strong typography, clean lines, and brand visuals, and it can output editable vector SVG straight from a prompt. Four modes. Standard for general work, Vector for logos and icons, Utility for product shots, Utility Vector for brand assets. Native vector.
